<div class='topics'>
<ul>
<li>
tspeed
</li>
<li>
ttime
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class='question question'>

Jason takes $4 \ \text{hours}$ to cycle $24 \ \text{kilometres}$. If Jason always cycles at the same speed, after how many hours has Jason cycled $15 \ \text{kilometres?}$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}     &=  \dfrac{24}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times 4}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
                 &=  6 \text{ kilometres/hour} \\\\
\text{Time taken}&=  \dfrac{15}{6} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{3 \times 5}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 5}{\cancel{3} \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{5}{2} \\\\
                 &=  2.5 \text{ hours or } 2 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.5 \text{ hours or } 2 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_formula_speed
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}     &=  \dfrac{24}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times 4}{4} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{6 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
                 &=  6 \text{ kilometres/hour} \\\\
\text{Time taken}&=  \dfrac{15}{6} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{3 \times 5}{3 \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 5}{\cancel{3} \times 2} \\\\
                 &=  \dfrac{5}{2} \\\\
                 &=  2.5 \text{ hours or } 2 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.5 \text{ hours or } 2 \text{ hours and } 30 \text{ minutes}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='papername'>
<p>st-albans-high-school-for-girls--11-plus--maths--2017--sample-paper-2:1:39</p>
</div>
<div class='rag'>

<li>

</li>

<div class='question question'>

Jacqueline and Sophia stand facing one another. At exactly the same moment both girls start to turn steadily on the spot.

It takes Jaqueline $3 \ \text{seconds}$ to make one full turn, whilst Sophia takes $4 \ \text{seconds}$ to complete one full turn.

How many times will Jaqueline have turned when the girls are next facing each other? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

LCM of $3,4 = 12$

Time taken $= 12 \text{ seconds}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of turns}  &=  \dfrac{12}{3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac{3 \times 4}{3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 4}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
                        &=  4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>LCM of $3,4 = 12$
<emptyline>
Time taken $= 12 \text{ seconds}$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of turns}  &=  \dfrac{12}{3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac{3 \times 4}{3} \\\\
                        &=  \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 4}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
                        &=  4
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='papername'>
<p>st-albans-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-2:1:15</p>

<div class='rag'>
<p>Waffles Woof</p>

tspeed
</li>

</div>
<div class='question question'>

A  space ship  travels  at  $193 \ \text{metres   per   second}$   for  $5  \ \text{minutes} \ 7 \ \text{seconds}$. Use   approximations to decide   whether  the   distance travelled   will  be nearer to $1 \ \text{km}, 10 \ \text{km}, 60 \ \text{km}$ or $600 \ \text{km}$. (You  must  show  your  working.) 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}  &=  193 \times (5 \text{ minutes } 7 \text{ seconds}) \\\\ 
                 &=  193 \times (5 \times 60 + 7) \\\\ 
                 &=  193 \times (300 + 7) \\\\ 
                 &=  193 \times 307 \\\\ 
                 &=  59251 \text{ metres} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  60000 \text{ metres} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60000}{1000} \text{ km} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60 \times 1000}{1000} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60 \times \cancel{1000}}{\cancel{1000}} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  60 \text{ km}
\end{aligned}
$

The distance travelled will be nearer to $60$ km.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$60$ km

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_formula_distance
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

                 &=  193 \times 307 \\\\ 
                 &=  59251 \text{ metres} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  60000 \text{ metres} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60000}{1000} \text{ km} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60 \times 1000}{1000} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  \dfrac{60 \times \cancel{1000}}{\cancel{1000}} \\\\ 
                 &\approx  60 \text{ km}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
The distance travelled will be nearer to $60$ km.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$60$ km
</code></pre>

Find out how far he runs in $4$ weeks.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Distance covered} &= 4 \times 4 \times 7 \\\\
                        &= 4 \times 28 \\\\
                        &= 112 \ \text{km}

$

<li class='questionContainer'>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If he takes $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to run $4 \ \text{km}$, what is his average speed in km 
per hour?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$


ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Speed}    &= \dfrac {4 \ \text{km}} {15  \ \text{minutes}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60 \ \text{km}} {15  \ \text{hours}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 15 \times 4} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{15} \times 4} {\cancel{15}} \\\\
                &= 4 \times 4 \\\\
                &= 16 \ \text{km per hour}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$16 \ \text{km per hour}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_formula_speed

$
\begin{aligned}

                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60 \ \text{km}} {15  \ \text{hours}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times 15 \times 4} {15} \\\\
                &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{15} \times 4} {\cancel{15}} \\\\
                &= 4 \times 4 \\\\
                &= 16 \ \text{km per hour}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

</div>

</code></pre>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha'>
<li class='questionContainer'>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Isobel cycles at a steady speed of $15 \ \text{miles per hour}$. Find the shortest time to complete the journey. 
Give your answer in minutes.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text {Speed} = \dfrac {\text{Distance}} {\text{Time}}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Isobel takes}     &= \dfrac {10} {15 \ \text {miles per hour}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15 \ \text {miles per min}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 3 \times 20} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 2 \times \cancel 3 \times 20} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 3} \\\\
                             &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                             &= 40 \ \text {min}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$40 \ \text {min}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_formula_speed
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time Isobel takes}     &= \dfrac {10} {15 \ \text {miles per hour}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15 \ \text {miles per min}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {10 \times 60} {15} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 3 \times 20} {5 \times 3} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 2 \times \cancel 3 \times 20} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 3} \\\\
                             &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
                             &= 40 \ \text {min}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$40 \ \text {min}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li class='questionContainer'>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Isobel listens to the local radio before setting off on her journey one morning. 
She discovers that the road between $E$ and $F$ is completely blocked due to an earlier accident 
and she is forced to choose an alternative route to work. 
What is the least number of **additional** minutes that she will take to cycle to work today?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

If $E$ and $F$ are blocked the shortest new route would be:

From $S \ \text {to} \ B \ \text {to} \ C \ \text{to} \ T$ .

Let's find the new distance.

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Distance}  &= S \ \text{to} \ B \ \text{to} \ C \ \text{to} \ T \\\\
                  &= 4 + 2 + 6 \\\\
                  &= 12 \ \text {miles} \\\\
\text {Speed}     &= 15 \ \text {miles per hour \ \ } \\\\
                  &= \dfrac {15} {60} \ \text {miles per min}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time} & = \dfrac {12} { \dfrac {15}{60} } \\\\
            & = 12  \times \dfrac {60} {15} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {12 \times 60} {15} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 5 \times 12} {5 \times 3} \\\\
            & = \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 4 \times \cancel 5 \times 12} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 3} \\\\
            & = 4 \times 12 \\\\
            & = 48 \ \text {min}                                                                   
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the additional minutes by finding the difference in time taken by previous route and the new route.

$48 \ \text {min}  - 40 \ \text {min} = 8 \ \text {min} $                                                                  

The additional minutes Isobel will take to sycel to work today $= 8 \ \text {min}$ 

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \ \text {min}$

</div>
</div>
